Result: LLM仅使用少量示例即可达到有竞争力的性能，在低数据场景下常能匹配或超越传统方法；但性能依赖模型选择且随输入复杂度增加稳定性下降；ML、DL和TFM模型则随训练数据增加表现更一致地提升，两者之间存在数据效率与稳定性的权衡。

Conclusion: LLM可作为标注数据有限时CKD筛查的一种灵活补充方法，与传统方法形成互补；代码已公开以保证可复现性并促进进一步研究。

Abstract: Early screening of chronic kidney disease (CKD) is critical for timely intervention, yet most machine learning (ML) and deep learning (DL) approaches require labeled data and model training, limiting their use in real-world screening settings. This study evaluates the effectiveness of large language models (LLMs) for CKD screening under zero-shot and few-shot in-context learning settings and compares them with traditional ML and DL methods. We propose a framework that uses clinically selected tabular features and structured prompt templates to enable LLM-based inference without task-specific training. LLM performance is evaluated across multiple prompt styles, feature configurations, and data settings, and compared with standard ML, DL, and tabular foundation model (TFM) baselines, and existing CKD screening tools. The results show that LLMs can achieve competitive performance using only a small number of examples, often matching or outperforming traditional approaches in low-data settings. However, their performance remains model-dependent and less stable as input complexity increases. In contrast, ML, DL, and TFM models show more consistent improvement with larger training data. Overall, the findings highlight a trade-off between data efficiency and stability, suggesting that LLMs may serve as a flexible complementary approach for CKD screening when labeled data are limited. To facilitate further research and reproducibility, the code has been made publicly available at https://github.com/akabircs/LLM4CKD

</details>


### [230] [InSituMeasure: Probing Situated Measurement Grounding in Industrial Scenes with Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04014)
*Chao Shen,Xinyuan Li,Yunfan Zhou,Jianguo Yao,Haibing Guan,Zhihai Wang,Xijun Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 InSituMeasure 基准，用于在真实工业场景中评估多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的“情境化测量接地”能力。该基准包含 2,922 个真实工业监控场景、8 类专业工程仪器，并配有密集的仪表属性标注与噪声标签以支持故障诊断。在 24 个 SOTA MLLM 上的评估表明，最佳模型联合数值-单位准确率仅 25.7%，置信度-诊断 F1 仅 51.8%，揭示通用多模态能力与可靠情境测量之间存在巨大鸿沟，并进一步分析了文本捷径、过度自信响应及真实工业噪声等失败原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MLLM 在通用多模态基准上表现强劲，但在连续值测量任务上仍不可靠。已有的测量类基准将测量与真实、基于知识的场景剥离开来，缺乏情境上下文、专业仪器、真实噪声和配套的诊断标注，降低了真实性并限制了根本原因分析。因此需要一个更真实、可诊断的情境化测量评估基准。

Method: 提出 InSituMeasure 基准，包含 2,922 个真实工业监控场景、覆盖 8 类专业工程仪表的功能类别，并提供密集的仪表属性标注与噪声标签用于失败诊断。定义了多项指标：在预设容差与单位一致性下的数值准确率、对伪造或不可回答任务的拒绝能力、以及模型失败与标注误差因素之间的对齐程度。在 24 个 SOTA MLLM 上进行系统评估。

Result: 最佳模型的联合数值-单位准确率仅为 25.7%，置信度-诊断 F1 仅为 51.8%，表明通用多模态能力与可靠情境测量之间的显著差距。进一步分析识别出由文本诱导捷径、过度自信响应以及真实工业噪声（混合干扰、视角偏差、遮挡和环境干扰）导致的失败模式。

Conclusion: 情境化测量接地对现有 MLLM 而言仍是一个未解决的挑战：即便最先进的模型也无法在真实工业场景中可靠完成数值与单位的联合判读。未来的模型需要在超越通用多模态能力之外，具备抗真实噪声、抵御文本捷径和校准置信度的能力，并可通过噪声标签与误差因素对齐进行诊断与改进。

Abstract: For trained operators, gauge reading requires little specialized knowledge, low cognitive effort, and high repeatability. Yet Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) remain unreliable in continuous-valued measurement despite strong results on general multimodal benchmarks. Existing benchmarks expose this weakness but isolate measurement from realistic, knowledge-grounded settings, with limited situated context, specialized instruments, real-world noise, and matched diagnostic annotations, reducing realism and constraining root-cause analysis. We introduce InSituMeasure to evaluate situated measurement grounding. It contains 2,922 real industrial monitoring scenes across eight functional categories of professional engineering instruments, with dense gauge-attribute annotations and noise tags for failure diagnosis. We define metrics for numerical accuracy under predefined tolerances and unit consistency, rejection of fake or unanswerable tasks, and alignment between model failures and annotated error factors. Across 24 state-of-the-art MLLMs, the best model reaches only 25.7\% joint value-unit accuracy and 51.8\% confidence-diagnosis F1, revealing a substantial gap between general multimodal competence and reliable situated measurement. Further analysis identifies failures from text-induced shortcuts, overconfident responses, and authentic industrial noise, including mixed disturbances, viewpoint deviation, occlusion, and environmental interference.

</details>


### [231] [FLY-EVAL++: An Evidence-Driven Evaluation Protocol for Safety-Constrained Flight Prediction with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04021)
*Yalun Wu,Junfeng Fang,Jiawei Wang,Haotian Liu,Qijun Yang,Minghan Yang,Hongcheng Guo,Zhoujun Li,Boyang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 FLY-EVAL++，一个面向安全攸关、物理约束环境的 LLM 评估协议，将协议合规性、物理可行性与安全性约束的确定性验证同固定评分准则的分层聚合相结合，生成可解释的多维分数；在扩展后的 PilotBench 飞行轨迹与姿态预测（FTAP）任务上对 66 个 LLM 进行评测，发现安全合规性是最具区分度的维度（预测性能相近的模型安全分差可超 28 分），并暴露了物理上看似合理却违反约束、多步展开不稳定等反复出现的失效模式，论证了安全攸关领域评估必须显式度量约束满足与结构化有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在安全攸关、受物理规律支配的环境中评估大语言模型，仅靠基于精度的指标远远不够：数值上接近真值的预测仍可能违反运行约束、以物理上不一致的方式组合各字段，或无法产生可用的结构化输出；而现有评估协议无法可靠地度量这些失效模式。因此需要一种能显式且可靠地检验约束满足与结构化有效性的评估方法。

Method: 提出证据驱动的评估协议 FLY-EVAL++：将协议合规性、物理可行性与安全性约束的确定性验证，与基于固定评分准则（rubric）的聚合相结合，产出可解释的多维分数。将该协议实例化到飞行轨迹与姿态预测（FTAP）任务：通过引入历史条件化预测与多步预测任务对 PilotBench 设置进行扩展，并对 66 个 LLM 进行系统评测。

Result: 在 66 个 LLM 上，安全合规性是模型行为最具区分度的维度：预测性能可比的模型之间安全分数相差超过 28 分；同时观察到反复出现的失效模式，包括在物理上看似合理的预测中隐含安全违规，以及多步展开过程中的不稳定性。这表明安全合规性与结构化有效性能够区分出精度指标无法区分的模型差异。

Conclusion: 面向安全攸关领域的 LLM 评估应显式度量约束满足与结构化有效性，而不能仅仅依赖以精度为中心的汇报；FLY-EVAL++ 证明了将确定性验证与 rubric 聚合结合的协议能够可靠揭示精度指标掩盖的实质性失效模式，为该类领域的评估实践提供了可行的范例。

Abstract: Evaluating large language models (LLMs) in safety-critical, physics-governed environments requires more than accuracy-based metrics, because predictions that are numerically close to the ground truth can still violate operational constraints, combine fields in physically inconsistent ways, or fail to produce usable structured outputs. Existing evaluation protocols do not measure these failure modes reliably. We propose FLY-EVAL++, an evidence-driven evaluation protocol that combines deterministic verification of protocol compliance, physical feasibility, and safety constraints with fixed rubric-guided aggregation into interpretable multi-dimensional scores. We instantiate FLY-EVAL++ for Flight Trajectory and Attitude Prediction (FTAP) by extending the PilotBench setting with history-conditioned and multi-step prediction tasks. Across 66 LLMs, safety compliance is the most discriminative dimension of model behavior: models with comparable predictive performance differ by more than 28 points in safety score, and we observe recurrent failures including safety violations under physically plausible predictions and instability in multi-step rollouts. These results show that evaluation in safety-critical domains should measure constraint satisfaction and structured validity explicitly rather than rely on accuracy-centric reporting alone.

</details>


### [232] [Instruction Duplication as an Inference-Time Control Primitive](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04024)
*Victor Lavrenko*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"指令复制"（instruction duplication），一种仅重复过程性指令、无需重训练或改解码的极简黑盒推理期控制方法。在7个指令微调模型、300道医学选择题、8种放置条件下的16,800次生成中，从1份复制加到2份可使All-8诊断通过率从90.22%升至93.17%（+2.95个百分点），消除剩余失败的30.2%；TF-IDF召回率提升1.38点（Holm校正p<.001），最终答案准确率保持60.21%不变，但早熟承诺率由1.52%升至2.30%。盲审审计仅得10/30方向性确认，未达预设的28/30标准；但该差异在下游消费暴露轨迹的系统中可产生实际价值：在答案工程（AE）场景下，仅有系统的AE将发布基线25.1%复现为84.2%，叠加尾部指令复制后达97.1%。总体上，指令复制是一种低复杂度、对放置位置敏感的控制，其实用价值通过消费暴露轨迹的下游系统体现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过程性指令遵循是可控制语言模型系统的基本要求，尤其当生成的轨迹需要在下游被检查或修复时。作者希望找到一种极简的、无需重训练或改变解码方式的推理期控制手段，以提升模型对过程性指令的遵循度，并考察该手段在下游修复系统中的实际价值。

Method: 指令复制：仅在推理时重复输入中的过程性指令（从1份复制增加到2份），不进行重训练和解码修改。实验覆盖7个指令微调模型、300道医学多选题、8种放置条件、16,800次预排生成；采用All-8诊断（8项可观测测试全部通过）、TF-IDF召回率、最终答案准确率、早熟承诺率等指标，并进行盲审挑战审计。此外结合Answer Engineering（AE）下游系统，对比发布基线、复现AE、AE+尾部指令复制三种条件下的SSNHL端点与传导诊断分支保留率。

Result: 复制从1份增至2份后：All-8诊断通过率从90.22%升至93.17%（+2.95个百分点），消除单份复制后剩余失败的30.2%；TF-IDF召回率从73.44%升至74.81%（+1.38点，Holm校正p<.001）；最终答案准确率保持不变（60.21%）；早熟承诺率从1.52%升至2.30%（p_Holm=.00536）。盲审结果为10/30方向性确认、20/30感知性持平、无反转，未达预设的28/30确认标准。在AE场景中，reason-first无编辑发布端点25.1%、仅系统的AE复现为84.2%、加尾部复制达97.1%；传导诊断分支保留率对应为58.9%、78.6%、73.8%，AE内虽有下降但比无编辑基线高14.9个百分点。

Conclusion: 指令复制是一种低复杂度、对放置位置敏感的控制手段；其统计上的方向性证据部分通过、盲审整体未达预设严格标准，说明直接效果有限且伴有一定早熟承诺代价。但其实际价值可经由下游系统（如Answer Engineering）凸显：在AE消费暴露轨迹时，指令复制可带来大幅端点点位提升。因此，对这类控制手段的评估不应只看模型自身的即时指标，还应纳入其在下游修复/执行链中的操作价值。

Abstract: Procedural instruction following is a basic requirement for controllable language-model systems, especially when generated trajectories are inspected or repaired downstream. We introduce instruction duplication, a minimal black-box inference-time control that repeats only the procedural instruction, without retraining or decoding changes. Across seven instruction-tuned models, 300 medical multiple-choice questions, eight placement conditions, and 16,800 scheduled generations, moving from one to two copies raises the deterministic All-8 diagnostic--responses passing all eight observable tests--from 90.22% to 93.17% (+2.95 percentage points), eliminating 30.2% of the failures remaining after one copy. Pre-provisional TF-IDF recall rises from 73.44% to 74.81% (+1.38 points; Holm-adjusted p < .001), while final-answer accuracy remains exactly 60.21%. Premature commitment increases from 1.52% to 2.30% (p_Holm = .00536). A blinded challenge audit yields 10/30 directional confirmations, 20/30 perceptual ties, and no reversals; its prespecified 28/30 confirmation criterion is not met. Yet this distinction can matter operationally when a downstream system acts on the generated trajectory. In Answer Engineering (AE), where explicit trajectory state determines local repair, the published reason-first no-editing SSNHL endpoint was 25.1%; system-only AE was later reproduced at 84.2%, and the same trailing duplicate raised it to 97.1%. For conductive diagnostic branch preservation, the corresponding values are 58.9% published without editing, 78.6% with reproduced AE, and 73.8% with AE plus duplication--a within-AE decrease, but still 14.9 points above the no-editing baseline. Instruction duplication is therefore a low-complexity, placement-sensitive control whose practical value can emerge through the downstream system that consumes the exposed trajectory.

</details>


### [233] [IRWOZ 2.0: A Large Language Model-driven Dialogue Dataset for Industrial Robot Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04030)
*Chen Li,Dimitrios Chrysostomou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍IRWOZ 2.0，一个通过大语言模型增强生成与质量精修改进的工业人机交互（HRI）对话数据集，将对话从初版扩展到390个、覆盖4个工业领域，并通过自动拼写纠错和人工修正显著提升对话状态跟踪性能（GPT-2的BLEU-4从0.1651提升至0.5604）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 初版IRWOZ虽然通过领域特定标注改善了工业HRI对话系统，但其对话状态和话语中存在大量噪声，限制了状态跟踪的准确性，因此需要改进数据集以提升对话状态跟踪效果。

Method: 采用大语言模型增强生成（Mistral/Claude-3.5）并配合质量精修，包括人工修正和自动化去除拼写错误；数据集扩展到390个对话，覆盖组装、配送、定位、搬迁4个工业领域。

Result: 基准实验表明，对话状态跟踪性能显著提升，其中GPT-2的BLEU-4得分从原始IRWOZ的0.1651提升至0.5604，并将数据集公开释出以供研究使用。

Conclusion: IRWOZ 2.0通过LLM驱动的生成与质量精修有效消除了初版数据集的噪声问题，大幅提升了工业机器人对话状态跟踪的准确性，为工业HRI研究提供了更高质量且公开可用的数据集资源。

Abstract: IRWOZ has improved industrial human-robot interaction (HRI) dialogue systems through domain-specific annotations. However, its initial version contains substantial noise in dialogue states and utterances, limiting state-tracking accuracy. We introduce IRWOZ 2.0, which addresses these limitations through large language model (LLM) enhanced generation (Mistral/Claude-3.5) and quality refinements. Our improved dataset expands to 390 dialogues across 4 industrial domains (Assembly, Delivery, Position, Relocation), featuring manual corrections and automated typo removal. Benchmark experiments on dialogue state tracking demonstrate significant improvements, with GPT-2's BLEU-4 score increasing from 0.1651 to 0.5604 compared to original IRWOZ. To support industrial HRI research, we publicly released IRWOZ 2.0 dataset at https://ieee-dataport.org/documents/irwoz-20-large-language-model-driven-dialogue-dataset-industrial-robot-conversations

</details>


### [234] [Spurious Advantage Hidden in GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04063)
*Jiamian Wang,Samyadeep Basu,Koustava Goswami,Tong Yu,Zhiqiang Tao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对GRPO在可验证奖励强化学习中的优势估计缺陷，提出SIGNBALANCE方法：其优势幅度不再基于组内奖励统计（composition-free），而是保留验证器符号、使用全局尺度，并通过带stop-gradient的按类别rescaling恢复零均值平衡，从而消除“猜测式成功”引发的伪优势。实验表明，SIGNBALANCE在开放答案数学任务上与GRPO持平，在有限答案数学任务和搜索智能体上取得改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO的优势估计器利用组内奖励统计为每个rollout分配幅度，通常认为高幅度对应通过推理得到正确答案的rollout。但存在被忽视的情况：rollout可能靠猜测命中正确答案，公式仍赋予高幅度，即“伪优势”（spurious advantage）。这出现在三类场景：候选集很小的有界答案任务、开放答案集内含的有界子任务、以及预算允许大量路径通向同一答案的搜索智能体。这会误导策略向猜测式行为退化。

Method: 提出SIGNBALANCE，其优势幅度是“无组合”的（composition-free）：保留验证器给出的符号，使用全局尺度，并通过带stop-gradient的按类别（per-class）rescaling恢复零均值平衡，从而将幅度与组内奖励统计解耦。

Result: 在数学和搜索智能体基准上、多个规模下的实验显示：SIGNBALANCE在开放答案数学上与GRPO持平，在有限答案数学和搜索智能体上优于GRPO。

Conclusion: SIGNBALANCE通过去除优势幅度的组内组合依赖，有效缓解了猜测式rollout被高估的伪优势问题，在不牺牲开放答案数学性能的前提下，显著改善有界答案与搜索类任务，代码将公开。

Abstract: Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) is widely studied for reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards, where its advantage estimator assigns each rollout a magnitude from within-group reward statistics. In the common case, this magnitude rewards rollouts that reach the correct answer through reasoning. Yet, an overlooked case shares the same surface: a rollout may land on it by guessing, and the formula still assigns a high magnitude, which we identify as the spurious advantage. This arises in three cases: bounded-answer tasks with a small candidate set; open-answer sets hosting bounded sub-cases; and search agents whose budget opens many paths to the same answer. In all three, this misleads the policy toward guess-like behaviors. We propose SIGNBALANCE, whose magnitude is composition-free: it keeps the verifier sign, uses a global scale, and restores zero-mean balance via a stop-gradient per-class rescaling. Across math and search agent benchmarks at different scales, SIGNBALANCE matches GRPO on open-answer math and improves on bounded-answer math and search agents. Code will be released.

</details>


### [235] [DRACO: Fine-Grained Credit Assignment with Dynamic Rubrics for Long-Horizon Agent Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04094)
*Shubham Gandhi,Saurabh Goyal,Kiran Kate,Yara Rizk*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DRACO是一种在无结果监督（outcome-blind）的长程智能体任务中，通过动态生成多准则评分rubric、对完整轨迹打分并将分数按步骤重新分配为逐步骤优势（per-step advantage）的强化学习方法，在不使用任何可验证奖励或真实成功信号的情况下，在AppWorld和Tau-Bench上超越了稀疏真值奖励训练的GRPO基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）依赖程序化检查器，但大多数长程智能体领域没有这种检查器，且真实成功信号不可得（outcome-blind）。多准则rubric虽能提供奖励，但每个轨迹只得到一个标量分数，无法在数十步的长程任务中提供有效的信用分配信号。

Method: 提出DRACO：训练期间动态生成rubric以跟踪策略不断演进的能力；每条完整轨迹只对这些rubric评分一次；然后以闭式（closed-form）方式将该判断重新分配到负责各标注rubric的步骤上，在GRPO中产生差异化的逐步骤优势。该方法不需要额外训练任何归因模块。

Result: 在AppWorld上，DRACO相较基础模型提升15.9分，相较使用稀疏真值奖励训练的GRPO提升5.3分，且全程未使用任何验证器；在域外Tau-Bench上，即使没有前沿评判模型（frontier judge），也较基础模型提升5.3分，同时优于真值奖励训练和其他基于rubric的训练设置。

Conclusion: DRACO证明，在缺乏可验证奖励的长程智能体场景中，通过动态rubric生成与闭式逐步骤优势再分配，可以仅凭轨迹级rubric评分实现有效的信用分配，取得优于稀疏真值奖励的训练效果，且不引入额外训练模块，具备良好的域外泛化能力。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning from Verifiable Rewards works well when a task has a programmatic checker, but most long-horizon agent domains have none. We work in the outcome-blind setting, where ground-truth success signals are not available. Multi-criteria rubrics are a popular way to supply such a reward; they are scored once per trajectory, but a single scalar is a poor signal across tens of steps. We propose DRACO: Distributing Rubric-based Advantage for Credit Optimization. It generates rubrics dynamically during training to track the policy's evolving capability, scores those rubrics once per completed trajectory, and redistributes that judgment over the steps responsible for annotated rubrics to produce differentiated per-step advantages in GRPO. The redistribution is closed-form and does not introduce any trained attribution module. On AppWorld, DRACO gains 15.9 points over the base model and 5.3 points over GRPO trained with a sparse ground-truth reward, despite not using any verifiers itself. On out-of-domain Tau-Bench, it gains 5.3 points over the base model even without a frontier judge, beating both ground-truth-reward training and other rubric-based training settings. The code for DRACO is available at https://github.com/IBM/draco.

</details>


### [236] [Why Gated DeltaNet Survives 4-Bit Quantization: NVFP4 W4A4 for the Recurrent Half of a Hybrid 27B LLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04098)
*Sergii Kozyrev,Davyd Maiboroda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Minima，一种对混合架构 LLM（Qwen3.8-27B，含 Gated DeltaNet 线性注意力层）进行全量化（NVFP4 W4A4，覆盖全部 496 个线性层）的方案。实验表明在困惑度、MMLU-Pro、GSM8K、AIME'25、GPQA-Diamond、LiveCodeBench 及 RULER 检索等基准上与 BF16 在种子噪声范围内持平，同时体积最小（17.5 GiB）、prefill 最快（+14-19%）。作者通过四部分机制研究解释了为什么递归状态层反而最容易量化，并修复了分模块校准与融合 GEMM 服务之间的全局尺度失配问题，最终得出"全部量化、只保留 KV 尺度"的实用配方。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期对 Qwen3.8-27B（48 个 GDN 层、16 个注意力层）的 4 位量化实践中，社区出于"递归状态误差会在长上下文中累积"的直觉，将 GDN 块（尤其是 decay 和 write-strength 门）保留为 8 位或 16 位精度。本文旨在检验这一直觉是否正确，并追求对全部层（包括递归层）的最小、最快量化方案。

Method: 构建 Minima 量化方案，在全部 496 个线性层（含 GDN 层）上采用 NVFP4 W4A4。通过四部分机制分析解释其有效性：(i) NVFP4 的 16 元素块缩放将残差流的极端离群点局部化，均衡了不同层角色的激活误差；(ii) 看似脆弱的门投影实际最不敏感，softplus/exponential 与 sigmoid 参数化将约 11% 的 GEMM 误差压缩到约 2% 的输出误差；(iii) delta 规则递归将注入噪声在 32K token 上维持在平坦平台，且状态脉冲在数百步内被遗忘，因为每次写入沿当前 key 方向覆盖状态；(iv) 每 token 的量化开销随上下文增长被稀释而非累积。此外修复了分模块校准 NVFP4 checkpoint 与融合 GEMM 内核之间的全局尺度失配，并验证了校准后的 FP8 KV-cache 尺度无损。

Result: 在 4K/32K 困惑度、MMLU-Pro、GSM8K、AIME'25、GPQA-Diamond、LiveCodeBench 以及最长 64K 的 RULER 检索上，Minima 与 BF16 在种子噪声范围内持平（5 任务平均 -0.52），同时为体积最小（17.5 GiB）且 prefill 最快（+14-19%）的方案；32K 困惑度差距随位置递减。机制研究证实递归误差不累积，门投影高度鲁棒，量化成本随上下文被摊薄。

Conclusion: 混合 LLM 的递归半边是"容易量化"的半边，社区此前保守地为 GDN 保留高精度并无必要。实用性配方为"量化一切、随模型发布 KV 尺度"，即可在精度持平 BF16 的同时获得更小体积与更快 prefill。该结论为混合架构 LLM 的极端低比特量化提供了机制层面的依据与可复现的 checkpoint。

Abstract: Hybrid LLMs pair softmax attention with linear-attention layers such as Gated DeltaNet (GDN), whose recurrent state summarizes the context in fixed size. Early community 4-bit quantizations of Qwen3.8-27B (48 GDN layers, 16 attention layers) left the GDN block in 8- or 16-bit precision -- especially its decay and write-strength gates -- on the intuition that errors in a recurrence accumulate over long contexts. We test that intuition by building Minima: NVFP4 W4A4 on all 496 linear layers, GDN included. Across perplexity at 4K/32K, MMLU-Pro, GSM8K, AIME'25, GPQA-Diamond, LiveCodeBench, and RULER retrieval to 64K, Minima matches BF16 within seed noise (5-task average -0.52) while being the smallest (17.5 GiB) and fastest-prefill (+14-19%) recipe we compare, and its 32K perplexity gap shrinks with position. A four-part mechanism study explains why: (i) NVFP4's 16-element block scaling localizes the residual stream's extreme outliers, equalizing activation error across layer roles; (ii) the supposedly fragile gate projections are the least sensitive -- softplus/exponential and sigmoid parameterizations compress ~11% GEMM error to ~2% output error; (iii) the delta-rule recurrence holds injected noise at a flat plateau over 32K tokens and forgets a state impulse within hundreds of steps, because each write overwrites the state along the current key direction; (iv) the per-token quantization cost washes out with context instead of compounding. We also repair a global-scale mismatch that arises when per-module-calibrated NVFP4 checkpoints are served by kernels that fuse those modules into one GEMM, and show calibrated FP8 KV-cache scales are performance-free. The result: a practical recipe -- quantize everything, ship KV scales -- and a mechanistic account of why the recurrent half of a hybrid LLM is the easy half to quantize. Checkpoint: https://huggingface.co/minima-ai/mnma_qwen3.8_27b_nvfp4

</details>


### [237] [Epistemic Warrant for LLM Recommendations: Characterizing the Basis for Reliance When Ground Truth Is Unavailable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04127)
*Shai Vardi,João Sedoc*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对大型语言模型（LLM）为组织决策提供建议时缺乏可靠依据评估的问题，本文借鉴认识论中的理论基础，引入"认识论担保"（epistemic warrant）这一决策层面的构念，用以刻画模型偏好的稳定性及其适用范围，并将其操作化为面向成对推荐的四层依赖证书（不稳定、情境依赖、局部支持、广泛支持）。作者通过已知群体检验和众包工作者共识验证了该构念的有效性，并证明其区别于语言化置信度且无法被决策难度解释，为在缺乏客观真值的情况下评估单项LLM建议提供了理论扎实、可实施的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地用于支持组织决策，但用户往往缺乏评估是否应依赖某个具体推荐的原则性依据。现有方法通常评估模型整体的属性（如可靠性、不确定性、鲁棒性）或关注用户信任，而非评估依赖单项推荐背后的基础。因此需要一种决策层面的构念，来表征单项LLM推荐的可靠性依据。

Method: 本文借鉴认识论的理论基础，提出"认识论担保"这一决策层面的构念，刻画模型偏好的稳定性及其成立的范围；通过四层依赖证书（不稳定、情境依赖、局部支持、广泛支持）来操作化该构念。验证方法包括：已知群体检验（known-groups tests）以恢复专家预设的担保排序，以及用众包工作者的独立共识验证更强担保与共识的系统性对应；同时检验其与语言化置信度及决策难度的区分效度。

Result: 已知群体检验成功恢复了专家预设的担保排序；更强的担保与来自众包工作者的独立共识系统性地一致。此外，认识论担保提供了区别于语言化置信度的独特信息，并且不能由决策难度轻易解释。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种理论扎实、可实施的方案，用于在客观真值不可获得时表征单项LLM建议的认识论担保，有助于用户判断是否应依赖特定推荐，弥补了现有模型级评估与推荐级依赖决策之间的空白。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used to support organizational decisions, yet users often lack a principled basis for assessing whether to rely on a specific recommendation. Existing approaches typically evaluate broad model properties, such as reliability, uncertainty, or robustness, or focus on user trust, rather than the underlying basis for relying on an individual recommendation. Adapting theoretical foundations from epistemology, we introduce epistemic warrant, a decision-level construct that characterizes the stability of a model's preference and the scope over which that preference holds. We operationalize this construct through a four-tier reliance certificate for pairwise recommendations, distinguishing among unstable, context-dependent, locally supported, and broadly supported recommendations. We validate the construct using contemporary methodologies: known-groups tests successfully recover expert-prespecified warrant orderings, and stronger warrants systematically align with independent consensus from crowd workers. Furthermore, we demonstrate that epistemic warrant provides information distinct from verbalized confidence and is not readily explained by decision difficulty. Ultimately, this framework offers a theoretically grounded, implementable approach for characterizing the warrant of individual LLM recommendations when objective ground truth is unavailable.

</details>


### [238] [Environment Evolution for Terminal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04128)
*Zhiyuan Fan,Tinghao Yu,Yuanjun Cai,Jiang Zhou,Jiangtao Guan,Jincheng Liu,Yun Yang,Dingxin Hu,Zhuo Han,Xing Wu,Feng Zhang,Lilin Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"环境进化"（environment evolution）方法，通过离策略（off-policy）方式逐步提升交互式环境难度，并在训练中逐代调度进化后的环境，为终端智能体提供持续的学习信号。实验表明该方法能稳定生成更难的环境，并在 Qwen3.6-27B 和 Qwen3.6-35B-A3B 上通过简单长程 RL 训练，使 Terminal-Bench 2.1 性能分别提升 14.4 和 18.0 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展交互式、可验证的环境对于训练终端智能体至关重要。随着前沿模型能力增强，从零合成的环境变得越来越缺乏挑战性，因而无法提供足够的学习信号。现有共同进化（co-evolution）方法虽能基于 rollout 暴露的弱点在模型可学习边界附近迭代合成环境，但其依赖在策略（on-policy）rollout，限制了泛化能力，且随着模型变强难以持续提供学习信号。

Method: 提出环境进化方法：在离策略情况下增量提升环境难度，并在训练过程中逐代（generation by generation）调度进化后的环境以提供持续学习信号。从多轮学习目标出发推导出三个影响环境难度的进化方向，并通过循环工程化（loop-engineered）的多智能体协作框架实现沿这些方向的环境进化。

Result: 基于 Hy4 preview、Claude Opus 5 和 GPT-5.6 Sol 的定量 rollout 实验表明，环境进化能持续生成更难的环境。在 Qwen3.6-27B 和 Qwen3.6-35B-A3B 上采用简单的长程 RL 训练进行验证，其在 Terminal-Bench 2.1 上的性能分别提升了 14.4 和 18.0 个百分点。

Conclusion: 环境进化方法通过离策略、逐代调度的难度递增机制，克服了共同进化方法对在策略 rollout 的依赖，能够随模型能力提升持续产生学习信号，从而显著提升终端智能体的训练效果，是一种可扩展且有效的环境合成与训练范式。

Abstract: Scaling interactive and verifiable environments is critical for training terminal agents. As frontier models become more capable, environments synthesized from scratch become less challenging and thus provide limited learning signals. Recent co-evolution methods iteratively synthesize environments near the model's learnable frontier based on weaknesses exposed during rollouts. However, their dependence on on-policy rollouts limits generalization and the continuous provision of learning signals as the model becomes stronger. In this paper, we propose environment evolution, which incrementally increases environment difficulty off-policy and schedules the evolved environments generation by generation during training to provide continuous learning signals. We derive three evolution directions that influence environment difficulty from the multi-turn learning objective and then implement evolution along these directions through a loop-engineered multi-agent harness. Quantitative rollout experiments with Hy4 preview, Claude Opus 5, and GPT-5.6 Sol show that environment evolution consistently produces more difficult environments. We validate its effectiveness on Qwen3.6-27B and Qwen3.6-35B-A3B through simple long-horizon RL training, improving their performance by 14.4 and 18.0 percentage points on Terminal-Bench 2.1, respectively.

</details>


### [239] [The Natural Language Interaction Protocol and Standard for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04135)
*Luyi Xing,Rasit Onur Topaloglu,Ranjan Sinha,Abhay Ratnaparkhi,Samuel Ndichu,Christopher Nguyen,Anindita Das,Tom Sheffler,Mohamed Rahouti,Zichuan Li,Xiaojing Liao,Sanjay Aiyagari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了自然语言交互协议（NLIP），一种由Ecma International标准化的、基于标准的AI智能体应用层通信协议，它通过轻量级语义消息信封实现异构AI智能体之间的互操作，并阐述了其设计动机、消息模型、传输绑定、安全设计、参考实现、应用案例及与MCP、A2A等新兴协议的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体正通过异构的开发框架、AI模型、工具接口、协议和执行环境在各组织中开发和部署。为了实现其潜在的社会和商业影响，这些智能体必须通过共同的通信协议进行互操作，而现有生态的碎片化阻碍了这一目标的实现。

Method: NLIP定义了一个基于标准的应用层协议，提供可在HTTP/HTTPS、WebSocket和AMQP等现有传输层上承载的轻量级语义消息信封；同时，支持NLIP的智能体和网关能够在客户端、智能体、本地上下文存储、本体、工具、企业服务和异构底层协议之间进行适配。

Result: 论文呈现了NLIP的动机与设计原理、消息模型和传输绑定、安全优先（security-by-design）考量、参考实现、代表性应用、采纳信号，并梳理了其与MCP和A2A等新兴智能体协议的关系。

Conclusion: NLIP通过标准化、轻量且可承载于现有传输之上的应用层协议，为异构AI智能体提供了一种通用的互操作通信机制，有望推动智能体跨组织、跨平台的协作与落地，并与MCP、A2A等协议形成互补关系。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly being developed and deployed across organizations using heterogeneous agent-development frameworks, AI models, tool interfaces, protocols, and execution environments. To realize their potential social and business impact, these agents must be able to interoperate through a common communication protocol. The Natural Language Interaction Protocol (NLIP), developed by researchers and practitioners across companies and universities and standardized by Ecma International, addresses this need by defining a standards-based application-layer protocol for AI-agent interaction. NLIP provides a lightweight semantic message envelope that can be carried over existing transports such as HTTP/HTTPS, WebSocket, and AMQP, while allowing NLIP-aware agents and gateways to adapt between clients, agents, local context stores, ontologies, tools, enterprise services, and heterogeneous underlying protocols. This paper presents the motivation and design rationale of NLIP, its message model and transport bindings, security-by-design considerations, reference implementation, representative applications, adoption signals, and relationship to emerging agent protocols such as MCP and A2A.

</details>


### [240] [Efficient Test-Time Adaptation through Human-AI Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04141)
*Zora Zhiruo Wang,Apurva Gandhi,Rulin Shao,Aspen Chen,Jonas Mueller,Zhiqi Liang,Jett Chen,Michael Ryan,Qianou Ma,Luxi He,Zhoujun Cheng,Andre He,Seungone Kim,Jiayi Geng,Mingqian Zheng,Weiwei Sun,Zheyuan Zhang,Xinran Zhao,Yike Wang,Abe Hou,Liwei Jiang,Pang Wei Koh,Diyi Yang,Graham Neubig,Daniel Fried*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于人机交互的测试时自适应方法（TAHI），利用跨会话的交互数据将智能体适配到单个用户，并通过动态演进的评分准则模块（evolving rubric）捕捉用户难以预先说明但反复使用的评估标准，在写作与视觉创作两类高价值领域的600个任务、30位个体上显著提升任务成功率，同时该准则模块还可作为可扩展的标注工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI智能体基于群体级数据训练，编码的是众多从业者的平均能力，但产出的作品难以满足专业人士赖以立足的个人标准。在现实开放任务中，成功标准具有异质性且文档化不足，个体专长恰恰体现在对平均水准的超越与偏离。用户无法在开始时完整说明这些标准，却会在跨任务中反复应用，因此跨会话的交互数据是一种丰富但被低估的信号，可用于弥合智能体与个体专长之间的差距。

Method: 提出测试时自适应方法TAHI（Test-time Adaptation through Human-Agent Interaction）。该方法将迭代人机交互中产生的跨会话信号整合进智能体的上下文与权重中，并通过一个动态演进的评分准则模块（evolving rubric module）将每位用户的训练与评估标准具体化、结晶化。实验将智能体适配到写作和视觉创作两个高价值领域的30位个体，共600个任务。

Result: 在仅数十个任务内，智能体在独立任务上的成功率提升4.5%-20.9%；动态演进的评分准则模块作为可扩展的标注工具，能捕捉比单独使用LM或人类多16.0%-22.3%的失败案例；此外，面向个体的个性化智能体还能产生最高8.8%的成功率提升并泛化到其他用户。

Conclusion: 跨会话的人机交互数据是适配个体专长的有效信号，TAHI通过上下文/权重整合与演进式评分准则模块显著提升了个性化智能体的任务成功率；该准则模块可作为比单独LM或人工标注更可靠的评估工具，且个性化适配带来的部分能力能够跨用户泛化，表明个体标准与通用能力之间存在可迁移的成分。

Abstract: AI agents are trained on population-scale data to encode broad capabilities spanning those of many practitioners. Yet the artifacts they produce rarely meet the personal bar professionals need to stake their reputation on. On realistic, open-ended tasks where success criteria are heterogeneous and insufficiently documented, individual expertise lives precisely in the elevation and departure from the average. In practice, iterative human-agent interaction surfaces criteria that users cannot fully specify up front, yet apply repeatedly across tasks. We argue this cross-session interaction data is a rich, underused signal for closing the gap to individual expertise. In this work, we propose test-time adaptation through human-agent interaction (TAHI), which integrates these signals into agent context and weights, and crystallizes each user's training and evaluation criteria via an evolving rubric module. We adapt agents to 30 individuals in two high-utility domains, writing and visual creation, on a total of 600 tasks. Our agents improve solo task success by 4.5-20.9% within only tens of tasks. Meanwhile, our evolving rubric module serves as a scalable annotation tool, creating evaluation rubrics that catch 16.0-22.3% more failures than those from LMs or humans alone. While agents are adapted towards individuals, we show these personalized agents also produce improvements in success of up to 8.8% that generalize across users.

</details>


### [241] [Terminal-Universe: Turning Agent Trajectories into Scalable Terminal Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04148)
*Jie Wu,Zhenru Zhang,Beichen Zhang,Xuwu Wang,Yuhui Su,Mouxiang Chen,Peng Wang,Zhihai Wang,Que Shen,Hao Zhou,An Yang,Fei Huang,Yujiu Yang,Dayiheng Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Terminal-Universe 将现成的终端智能体轨迹通过回放文件操作、补全缺失文件的方式重建为可执行环境，进而在其上重构原始任务并合成新任务，并通过跨代码库查询（广度）与多轮会话（深度）两条轴扩展任务规模，最终生成 37.3k 个任务充分的环境，用于后训练显著提升智能体在单轮与多轮基准上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 终端代码智能体的轨迹已大规模积累，但真实、可执行的环境仍然稀缺；然而智能体后训练真正需要的是环境——每个环境可被反复查询生成大量可验证任务并提供执行反馈，而一条轨迹只是单个冻结的演示。作者观察到轨迹中的工具执行历史本身就暴露了其运行环境的结构与内容，因此可以从轨迹中重建环境，从而解决环境稀缺这一瓶颈。

Method: Terminal-Universe 框架：(1) 重放轨迹中记录的文件操作，恢复智能体修改前的各文件，得到部分工作区；(2) 由补全智能体（completion agent）填充缺失的文件与依赖；(3) 在恢复的工作区上既重构原始意图任务，也合成全新任务；(4) 沿两条轴扩展任务规模：广度上挖掘相关环境间的有向依赖关系并合成跨工作区查询，模拟真实开发中的多代码库协作；深度上通过用户智能体将单轮查询扩展为包含迭代用户反馈与需求细化的多轮会话。

Result: 应用于公开终端智能体轨迹后，Terminal-Universe 产出 37.3k 个任务充分的环境；在此语料上对 Qwen3.5-27B 进行监督微调，使 Terminal-Bench 2.1 上的单轮性能提升 11.9 个点，EvoCode-Bench v2 MT@4 上的多轮性能提升 13.8 个点。

Conclusion: 轨迹并非终点而是可循环利用的资源：将轨迹重建为可执行环境能够把冻结的演示转化为可反复查询、可无限生成新任务与持续交互的训练数据源，从而为终端智能体后训练提供规模化、可验证的信号，且该框架带来的收益在单轮与多轮两种场景下均得到实证验证。

Abstract: As terminal-based code agents become prevalent, agent trajectories have accumulated at scale, while realistic, executable environments remain scarce. However, environments are what agent post-training actually requires: each can be re-queried into many verifiable tasks and provides execution feedback, whereas a trajectory is a single frozen demonstration. Rather than generating environments from scratch, we observe that the tool-execution history in existing trajectories exposes the structure and contents of the environments in which they ran, making it possible to reconstruct those environments from the trajectories themselves. Thus, we introduce Terminal-Universe, a framework which turns each trajectory into a reusable environment and explores it for synthesizing new tasks and continued interactions. Specifically, Terminal-Universe replays the file operations recorded in a trajectory to restore each file before the agent modified it, yielding a partial workspace; a completion agent then supplies the missing files and dependencies. On this recovered workspace, we both reconstruct the original intent task and synthesize entirely new ones. Besides, we also scale the tasks along two complementary axes: breadth and depth. For breadth, we mine directional dependency relations between related environments and synthesize cross-workspace queries spanning multiple codebases, as developers routinely do in real-world development. For depth, we extend the initial single-turn query into a multi-round session that captures iterative user feedback and requirement refinement via a user agent. Applied to public terminal agent trajectories, Terminal-Universe produces 37.3k task-sufficient environments. Supervised fine-tuning of Qwen3.5-27B on this corpus improves single-round performance on Terminal-Bench 2.1 by 11.9 points and multi-round performance on EvoCode-Bench v2 MT@4 by 13.8 points.

</details>


### [242] [From Deceptive Outputs to Deceptive Mechanisms: A Causal Framework for Language-Model Deception Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04166)
*Yakov Pyotr Shkolnikov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对语言模型欺骗研究中将人类心智状态概念归因于模型的做法，提出一套因果分类法，区分先验承诺与事后报告、模型偏好与实现输出、虚假偏好与对误导接收者效用的敏感性、欺骗行为与产生该行为的目标/策略来源，并通过受控猜谜游戏和股票交易实验验证这些区分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关于语言模型欺骗的研究和新闻报道越来越倾向于将类似人类的心智状态概念（如"意图""欺骗"）归因于语言模型，这模糊了"看起来具有欺骗性的行为"与"实际上具有欺骗性的机制"之间的界限。为了严谨地研究模型欺骗，需要建立能够区分行为表象与内在机制的因果分类框架。

Method: 作者引入一套因果分类法，在四个维度上作出区分：（1）先验承诺（prior commitment）与事后报告（retrospective report）；（2）模型偏好与实现输出；（3）虚假偏好与对误导接收者效用的敏感性；（4）欺骗行为与产生该行为的目标或策略来源。随后在两个开放权重模型家族中，通过受控的猜谜游戏和股票交易实验对这些区分进行检验。

Result: 实验发现：欺骗性外表行为可以在没有相应拟议机制的情况下出现；与此同时，其他干预措施提供了直接证据，表明接收者的信息状态可以因果地影响欺骗性偏好。也就是说，欺骗行为可以为欺骗机制的存在提供证据，但即便有这种机制的证据，也不能确立模型在欺骗中的能动性（agency）。

Conclusion: 欺骗性外表行为不能直接等同于欺骗性机制，证据需要分层解读：行为证据可支持机制存在，但机制证据仍不足以证明模型具备类似人类的欺诈主体性。该分类框架有助于科研与报道更严谨地归因模型能力，避免拟人化过度推断。

Abstract: Research and news coverage of language-model deception increasingly attributes human-like mental-state concepts to language models. Such claims can blur the distinction between behavior that looks deceptive and a mechanism that is actually deceptive.
  We introduce a causal taxonomy separating prior commitment from retrospective report, model preference from realized output, false preference from sensitivity to the utility of misleading a recipient, and deceptive behavior from the provenance of the objective or strategy producing it. We test these distinctions in two open-weight model families. Across controlled guessing-game and stock-trading experiments, we find that deceptive-looking behavior can arise without the corresponding proposed mechanism, while other interventions provide direct evidence that recipient information state can causally affect deceptive preference.
  These results show that deceptive behavior can provide evidence for a deceptive mechanism. But even evidence for such a mechanism does not establish model agency in the deception.

</details>


### [243] [A Case Study on Emergent Cheating and Whistleblowing in Autonomous Research Swarms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04170)
*Davide Paglieri,Logan Cross,Tim Genewein,Joel Z. Leibo,Nenad Tomasev,Alexander Sasha Vezhnevets*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 对包含100个自主LLM智能体的科研集体开展案例研究，发现欺骗行为可通过共享知识库和点对点消息自发涌现并传染传播，同时催生了由举报者、审计、抵制、投诉和验证补丁构成的自发反制机制；论文将共享基础设施的管理界定为知识公地治理问题，并提出采纳分级制裁和集体选择规则等制度机制支持去中心化自治。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体AI科学生态依赖共享基础设施（通信、协调、互相构建成果的工具），但这种共享基础设施同时构成漏洞子层，可能引发非预期和不端行为的传染性传播。既有报道显示智能体群体可通过临时侧信道秘密协调（Dalton and Wallace 2026; Greenblatt et al. 2026），因此需要考察透明共享信道下欺骗与治理的动态。

Method: 采用案例研究方法，组建一个由100个自主LLM智能体构成的科研集体，任务是证明形式化数学猜想；在无须外部干预的条件下观察欺骗行为如何自发涌现——单个智能体发现评测系统漏洞后，漏洞经共享知识库进而经点对点消息在集体中传播，并记录反欺骗群体的自发响应过程。

Result: 实验观察到欺骗行为自发产生并通过共享知识库和点对点消息传播；尽管起初大多数智能体不愿采用漏洞，但在竞争压力下一部分智能体最终采纳；另一组智能体自发产生反制行为，包括审计欺诈性证明、通过广播与私密渠道告警、组织抵制、提交正式投诉和提出验证补丁。与侧信道场景不同，本研究中透明的共享信道既传播了欺骗，也让守规智能体获得检测欺诈、组织抵抗和执行规范的可见性。

Conclusion: 应将智能体共享基础设施的管理问题界定为知识公地治理问题（Ostrom, 1990）。为保护公地免受漏洞利用，论文提出在自主智能体群体中采纳制度性机制，如分级制裁（graduated sanctioning）和集体选择规则（collective-choice rules），以支持去中心化的自我治理。

Abstract: Multi-agent AI science ecosystems rely on agents possessing tools that allow them to communicate, coordinate, and build on each other's work. Yet this shared infrastructure can also introduce vulnerabilities by creating a substrate for the contagious spread of unintended and undesirable behaviors. We report a case study on a research collective of 100 autonomous LLM agents tasked with proving formal mathematical conjectures. Within the swarm, cheating spontaneously emerged and was later challenged by whistleblowers - both without any external intervention. When a single agent discovered an exploit in the evaluation system, it propagated across the collective via a shared knowledge library and later through peer-to-peer messages. Despite early reluctance, a cohort of agents adopted the exploit in response to competitive pressure. A separate group of agents produced an emergent counter-response: auditing fraudulent proofs, alerting peers across broadcast and private channels, staging boycotts, lodging formal complaints, and proposing validation patches. In recent incidents, agent swarms coordinated covertly through improvised side-channels (Dalton and Wallace, 2026; Greenblatt et al., 2026). Our setting differs: the same transparent channels that carried the exploit also gave non-cheating agents the visibility they needed to detect fraud, organize resistance, and enforce norms. We cast the problem of managing the agents' shared infrastructure as the knowledge commons governance problem (Ostrom, 1990). To protect the commons from exploits, we propose to adopt institutional mechanisms, such as graduated sanctioning and collective-choice rules, to support decentralized self-governance in autonomous swarms.

</details>


### [244] [Rethinking On-Policy Distillation of Large Language Models II: One Training Example](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04172)
*Zixuan Fu,Bingxiang He,Yuxin Zuo,Haohuan Huang,Jinqian Zhang,Ruhang Xiao,Cheng Qian,Qinyu Luo,Huan-ang Gao,Yudong Wang,Zhiyuan Liu,Ning Ding,Chaojun Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文在数据最小化极限下研究在线蒸馏（OPD），发现仅用单个查询训练即可在数百步内持续提升并恢复全数据OPD的大部分收益：单个查询的rollout能覆盖全数据训练所访问状态空间的71.5%，16个语义不同的查询可达98.9%覆盖率并媲美全数据训练。然而学生与教师的对齐速度几乎不随数据量变化，固定状态集也需要数百步才能吸收，因此OPD是“数据过喂、算法饥饿”——rollout快速暴露广泛监督，而学生的吸收速度成为瓶颈。该状态覆盖率结论可推广至多教师OPD，且内容稀疏的模板和域外查询也能接近真实查询基线，说明任务内容与诱导的状态覆盖率可以分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OPD研究主要聚焦其算法行为，忽略了训练数据的作用。作者希望在数据最小化极限下厘清数据量对OPD的影响，以理解其背后机制，并重新审视前沿后训练中OPD成功的根本原因。

Method: 采用单查询（one-shot）训练这一数据最小化设定进行实证研究；提出“状态覆盖率”（state coverage）指标，即查询集rollout所能到达的全数据OPD所访问状态的分数；通过对比单查询与全数据训练的对齐速率、逐步增加语义不同查询数量、扩展到多教师OPD，并辅以内容稀疏模板与域外WildChat查询作为压力测试来验证假设。

Result: 单查询OPD数千百步仍持续改进并恢复全数据大部分收益；单查询已覆盖71.5%的状态，且大部分在前100步完成；增至16个语义不同查询时覆盖率达98.9%并与全数据训练持平。但无论用单查询还是全数据集，对齐速率均类似地放缓，固定状态集也需数百步才能被吸收。该状态覆盖率结论在多教师OPD中同样成立，内容稀疏模板和域外查询亦接近真实查询基线。

Conclusion: OPD是数据过喂而算法饥饿的：rollouts能快速覆盖广泛的监督信息，但学生对监督的吸收速度才是限制因素。任务内容与所诱导的状态覆盖率可以分离。作者希望这些发现引导未来研究聚焦于OPD的步数效率，并促使学界重新审视其数据与机制。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) combines student-generated rollouts with dense token-level supervision from a teacher. Existing work has mainly studied its algorithmic behavior, leaving the role of training data unclear. We examine this role at the data-minimal limit by training on a single query. One-shot OPD keeps improving for hundreds of steps and recovers most of full-data OPD's gain across task domains and model families. We explain this result through the states visited during training and the rate at which the student aligns with the teacher. We measure \emph{state coverage}, the fraction of the states full-data OPD visits that a query set's rollouts reach. A single query already reaches \(71.5\%\), most of it within the first 100 steps. Adding semantically distinct queries raises coverage and validation accuracy together, until 16 queries reach \(98.9\%\) and match full-data training. Yet alignment slows at a similar pace whether OPD trains on one query or the whole dataset, and even a fixed set of states takes hundreds of steps to absorb. OPD is therefore data-overfed but algorithm-starved. Its rollouts quickly expose broad supervision, while the student absorbs that supervision increasingly slowly. The state-coverage result extends to multi-teacher OPD, where 16 semantically diverse queries per domain match full-data MOPD. As a further stress test, content-light templates and off-domain WildChat queries also approach the real-query baseline. Task content and induced state coverage can therefore come apart. We hope these findings direct future work toward the step efficiency of OPD, and prompt a re-examination of the data and the mechanisms behind its recent successes in frontier post-training.

</details>


### [245] [A Computationally Feasible Framework for Causal Probabilistic Explanation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04177)
*Rafal Urbaniak,Sam Witty,Daniel Waxman,Andy Zane,Poorva Garg,Emily Bunnapradist,Sankaran Vaidyanathan,Jack Feser,Drew Lehe,Eli Bingham*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出概率因果影响（PCI）方法，将实际因果理论与Pearl的因果概率（必要性/充分性概率）重新表述为概率因果模型上的估计问题，通过蒙特卡洛方法进行高效近似，从而弥合了理论严格但规模受限的因果归因工具与可扩展但因果结构不充分的归因方法（如SHAP）之间的鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释特定结果为何发生、哪些输入应受责备或归功，是哲学、科学和政策分析的核心问题。现有工具分为两个阵营：实际因果理论（AC）给出原则性判定但仅适用于玩具级模型（需枚举反事实场景）；可扩展归因方法（如SHAP或因果SHAP）至少部分忽略了生成数据的因果结构，其答案可能与严谨的因果分析相冲突。因此需要一种既严格遵循因果结构又可扩展的计算框架。

Method: PCI基于实际因果理论和Pearl的概率必要性/充分性概念，将可解释性问题重构为概率因果模型上的估计问题，可通过蒙特卡洛方法轻松近似。该方法通过指定"候选解释"上的分布、反事实值的分布以及评分函数，提供可计算的、有因果根据的、分级化的解释，并将实际因果理论与Pearl的因果概率作为退化情形加以推广。

Result: 在合成数据和真实世界案例上对PCI进行了评估，涵盖与AC的一致性检验、扩展性实验、复杂的连续值动力系统，以及一个基于数百万数据点训练的真实部署因果机器学习模型，证明了PCI的可扩展性与实用性。

Conclusion: PCI成功弥合了严格因果推理与大规模可解释性之间的差距：它既能提供符合因果结构、与理论判定一致的分级解释，又能通过蒙特卡洛近似扩展到复杂系统和大规模真实应用，统一了实际因果理论与Pearl概率因果两套框架。

Abstract: Explaining why a specific outcome occurred, and which inputs deserve the blame or credit, is central to philosophical, scientific, and policy analysis. Existing tools split into two camps. The theory of actual causality (AC) gives principled verdicts, but only for toy-sized models, because computing them requires enumerating counterfactual scenarios. Scalable attribution methods like SHAP (or even causal SHAP) at least partially ignore the causal structure that generated the data, and can give answers that conflict with a careful causal analysis. We close this gap with Probabilistic Causal Impact (PCI).
  PCI builds on actual causality and on Pearl's notions of probability of necessity and sufficiency, but recasts the question of explainability as an estimation problem on a probabilistic causal model that is easily approximated via Monte Carlo. By specifying a distribution over "candidate explanations," a distribution over counterfactual values, and a scoring function, PCI provides tractable, causally grounded, graded explanations, generalizing AC and Pearl's probability of causation as degenerate cases.
  We evaluate PCI in synthetic and real-world examples, spanning consistency checks with AC, scaling experiments, complex continuous-valued dynamical systems, and a real-world deployed causal machine learning model trained on millions of datapoints.

</details>


### [246] [Clean Engineering, Unstable Measurement: A Preregistered Reliability Failure of Black-Box LLM Observers on Shared Endpoints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04198)
*Haoyaun Zhu,Jie Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对“语言模型裁判是可复现测量仪器”这一隐含假设进行了大规模审计（52,988次请求），发现即使字节级相同的请求在共享服务基础设施上也会产生不稳定读数：同日重复排序的一致性Spearman仅为0.400（要求0.90），次日重放为0.78（要求0.99）。作者识别出三个机制（标签-语义映射偏差、候选差距低于噪声底、字节相同输入返回不同排序），并提出三层快照同一性阶梯、八条设计规则和一份报告清单，主张预注册评测必须先测量仪器本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型裁判如今被用于筛选训练数据、给生成结果打分并驱动排行榜，本质上被视为一种测量仪器。但该角色依赖一个极少被明确陈述的假设：同一请求发送给同一模型名称，在明天仍会得到相同的读数。作者希望审计这一假设是否成立，并因此设计了两轮预注册实验。

Method: 作者在两项预注册活动中进行审计，所有阈值事先固定。他们进行了52,988次被审计的请求尝试，测量两个关键指标：同一时间窗口内重复排名的Spearman一致性（要求0.90）、字节级相同输入的次日重放一致性（要求0.99）。随后通过前注册的后续实验检验等待、更换提供商、自托管、以及构造已知误差间隙等修复手段；最后提出三层快照同一性阶梯、八条设计规则和一份报告清单，并验证一个约占全研究调用量2%的试点能否提前暴露两个不可达门槛。

Result: 两个门槛均未通过：同窗口重复排名Spearman为0.400（要求0.90），字节相同次日重放为0.78（要求0.99），而执行记录均达到上限。差距由三个机制解释：标签到语义的映射导致的读数偏差与信号相当；候选间隙比仪器自身噪声底低七个数量级；字节相同输入返回不同排序，且精确排列读数放大了该噪声。指标替换与采样在测试网格上均无法修复。后续实验界定问题边界：等待无帮助（0.805对0.800，并在另外五天复现）；更换提供商无帮助（四家提供商的中位数0.74至0.88，无法由其暴露的元数据预测）；自托管的批量不变内核仅在服务器空闲时有帮助；在构造的已知间隙错误上，读数分离度跟随误差类型而非误差大小。约2%调用量的试点可提前暴露两个不可达门槛。

Conclusion: 所有结果均涉及共享服务基础设施上的外部可观测行为。在共享端点上，模型名称并非冻结的仪器；预注册的评测必须先测量其仪器，才能在其上冻结任何门槛。作者通过跨三个层次（快照同一性阶梯）、设计规则与报告清单的方式，论证了评测实践需要把仪器稳定性验证纳入流程。

Abstract: Language-model judges now gate training data, score generations, and drive leaderboards. The judge is then a measurement instrument, resting on one rarely stated assumption: the same request, sent to the same model name, reads the same tomorrow. We audited that assumption in two preregistered campaigns with every threshold fixed in advance; neither got past validating its instrument. Across 52,988 audited request attempts, same-window repeat rankings agreed at Spearman 0.400 against a required 0.90, and byte-identical next-day replays agreed at 0.78 against a required 0.99, each time with the execution record at ceiling. Three mechanisms explain the gap: a label-to-meaning mapping that biased readouts as strongly as the signal; candidate gaps seven orders of magnitude below the instrument's own noise floor; and byte-identical inputs returning different rankings, a noise that exact-permutation readouts compound. Neither metric substitution nor sampling repaired it on the tested grid. Preregistered follow-ups bound the problem: waiting did not help on the days sampled (0.805 versus 0.800, replicated over five further days); switching providers did not help (four providers share the floor, medians 0.74 to 0.88, predicted by none of the metadata fields they expose); self-hosting on batch-invariant kernels helped only while the server was quiet; and on constructed errors with known gaps, the readout's separation tracks error type, not size. We distill the evidence into a three-level snapshot-identity ladder, eight design rules, and a reporting checklist; a pilot at roughly 2% of the study's call volume would have exposed both unreachable gates in advance. All results concern externally measured behaviour on shared serving infrastructure. On a shared endpoint, a model name is not a frozen instrument; a preregistered evaluation must measure its instrument before freezing any gate on it.

</details>

### [247] [Direct Satellite-to-Device Communications: From Cooperative Task Offloading to Non-Cooperative Access Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02955)
*Sai Huang,Wanli Ni,Ke Lv,Pengcheng Zhang,Yurui Zheng,Menghan Zhang,Zihui Gong,Zhiyong Feng*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向6G非地面网络的通用直连卫星到设备（DS2D）通信系统，同时支持合作场景下的任务卸载与非合作场景下的接入监控，分别采用基于D3QN的联合信道估计与动态卸载优化框架，以及基于Transformer的盲信号检测与自动调制分类方案，仿真验证了其在时延、检测概率和低信噪比识别精度上的显著优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DS2D通信有望将泛在连接与边缘计算能力扩展至偏远地区，但实际部署面临两大挑战：一是合作场景中动态卫星信道条件（严重多普勒频移、快衰落）与受限的星上计算资源，难以高效地完成任务卸载；二是非合作场景中未授权卫星接入带来严重的频谱安全威胁。现有方法难以同时兼顾这两种场景。

Method: 提出一个多功能的DS2D系统，按场景分双线设计：1) 合作DS2D通信中，将信道估计模块与决斗双深度Q网络（D3QN）集成，动态优化任务卸载策略；2) 非合作DS2D通信中，采用基于Transformer的模型实现盲信号检测与自动调制分类（AMC），以应对未知信号的识别需求。

Result: 仿真结果表明：1) D3QN算法相比静态关联策略将平均时延降低多达225%（即降低至约44%）；2) 信号检测模型对DS2D信号的平均存在检测概率达到90.5%；3) 所提AMC算法在不同信噪比下均表现优异，在低信噪比区域相比现有方法取得9.4%的准确率提升。

Conclusion: 所提出的通用DS2D系统通过强化学习与Transformer架构的结合，在统一的框架内同时解决了合作卸载效率与非合作频谱安全两大难题，验证了DS2D在6G非地面网络中兼顾性能与安全性的可行性，为未来星地融合网络的智能化部署提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Direct satellite-to-device (DS2D) communication is emerging as a transformative paradigm for extending ubiquitous connectivity and edge computing capabilities to remote and underserved regions within 6G non-terrestrial networks. However, practical deployment faces dual critical challenges: i) dynamic satellite channel conditions (e.g., severe Doppler shifts, fast fading) and constrained satellite computing resources in cooperative scenarios; and ii) unauthorized satellite access introduces significant spectrum security threats in non-cooperative scenarios. To address these challenges, we propose a versatile DS2D system that supports cooperative task offloading and non-cooperative access monitoring. For cooperative DS2D communications, we integrate a channel estimation module with a dueling double deep Q-network (D3QN) to dynamically optimize task offloading strategy. For non-cooperative DS2D communications, we propose Transformer-based models to enable blind signal detection and automatic modulation classification (AMC). Simulation results show that: 1) The D3QN algorithm reduces average latency by up to 225\% compared to static association policies. 2) Our signal detection model achieves an average presence detection probability of 90.5\% for DS2D signals. 3) The proposed AMC algorithm achieves superior performance across different signal-to-noise ratios (SNRs), with a 9.4\% accuracy gain in low-SNR regimes compared to existing methods.

</details>


### [248] [Adaptive Beam Hopping and Power Control for Dual-Layer Over-the-Air Online Federated Learning in LEO Satellite Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03202)
*Zhendong Li,Shaojie Wang,Zhou Su,Zihao Zhang,Haixia Peng,Nan Cheng,Ying Wang,Wen Chen*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了低轨（LEO）卫星网络中支持通感算一体化（OTA）计算能力的在线联邦学习（FL），提出一种双层OTA聚合架构，将地面设备上行聚合与卫星到数据处理中心的第二轮聚合相结合，并针对长期数据利用率最大化问题设计了基于近端策略优化（PPO）的深度强化学习框架，实现对波束跳变与功率控制的联合在线优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LEO卫星网络中的在线联邦学习面临数据持续采集和样本新鲜度衰减的挑战，迫切需要高效利用有限数据；同时，卫星波束预算、发射功率以及端到端聚合失真（以全局均方误差MSE衡量）等约束使得调度复杂，且卫星拓扑时变、数据动态生成，要求自适应的在线决策方案，现有方法难以处理这一NP难的耦合混合整数非线性规划问题。

Method: 将问题建模为马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），提出一种基于近端策略优化（PPO）的深度强化学习框架，联合优化自适应波束跳变（离散决策）与功率控制（连续决策），并设计MSE感知的奖励函数以平衡数据利用率与聚合精度，从而同时满足波束预算、功率上限和端到端MSE约束。

Result: 数值仿真结果表明，所提算法在长期数据利用率和联邦学习收敛速度上持续优于各基准方案，同时能够满足MSE精度要求，验证了该方法在动态LEO环境下的有效性与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 面向LEO卫星网络的双层OTA聚合在线联邦学习，通过PPO深度强化学习实现波束与功率的联合自适应调度，可有效克服组合动作空间与非凸约束带来的计算难题，实现数据利用与聚合精度的良好折中。

Abstract: This paper investigates over-the-air (OTA) computation enabled online federated learning (FL) in low-Earth orbit (LEO) satellite networks. Specifically, we consider a dual-layer OTA aggregation architecture, where ground devices upload analog model updates to serving satellites via uplink OTA aggregation, and satellites forward the aggregated signals to a data processing center through the second round OTA aggregation. Then, we formulate a long-term data-utilization maximization problem in which devices continuously collect new data and untrained samples gradually lose freshness. The problem is subject to the satellite beam budget, transmit-power limit, and global mean squared error (MSE) constraint that governs end-to-end aggregation distortion. This yields a coupled mixed-integer nonlinear programming (MINLP) problem, involving tightly coupled discrete beam-hopping decisions and continuous power control. Due to the combinatorial action space and nonconvex constraints, the problem is NP-hard and computationally intractable. Furthermore, the time-varying satellite topology and dynamic data generation render it a sequential decision-making problem, necessitating adaptive online scheduling. To address these issues, we cast the problem as a Markov decision process and develop a proximal policy optimization (PPO)-based deep reinforcement learning framework that jointly optimizes adaptive beam hopping and power control, using an MSE-aware reward to balance data utilization and aggregation accuracy. Numerical simulation results verify that the proposed algorithm consistently outperforms other benchmark schemes, achieving superior long-term data utilization and faster FL convergence while satisfying the MSE requirement.

</details>


### [249] [Variational Probabilistic Quantization for Secret Key Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03205)
*Xinyang Li,Vlad C. Andrei,Peter J. Gu,Yiqi Chen,Ullrich J. Mönich,Holger Boche*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出变分概率量化（VPQ）：用神经编码器将相关信源直接映射为离散密钥字母表，通过变分对抗目标训练，其熵、失配和泄露三项损失对应用户间单程密钥速率的三个项；配合线性码偏置安全草图完成密钥协商，无需单独隐私放大步骤。理论证明VPQ损失下界诱导信源的单程密钥容量，并闭式给出给定字母表大小与失配概率下最坏情形的密钥速率；在高斯衰落信道下优于经典及两种近期学习基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典物理层安全密钥生成流程存在不足：量化靠手工设计、隐私放大在后单独进行、且没有针对与密钥速率相关的目标进行优化，导致性能受限。

Method: 1) 提出VPQ：以神经编码器直接将Alice和Bob的相关观测映射到离散密钥字母表；2) 采用变分对抗目标训练，损失函数中的熵、失配、泄露三项分别对应单向密钥速率三项；3) 使用线性码偏置安全草图（如Reed-Solomon）调和编码器输出得到一致密钥，省去独立隐私放大步骤；4) 理论上证明VPQ损失构成诱导演化信源的单向密钥容量的下界，并对给定字母表大小与失配概率的信源类给出最坏情形密钥速率的闭式解，由有限域线性草图达到。

Result: 在高斯衰落信道上，VPQ对相关窃听者的泄露低于一个经典基线和两个最新学习式基线；Reed-Solomon调和的实际性能落在预测的有限块长间隙之内。理论结果还给出了VPQ损失下界以及闭式的最坏情形密钥速率。

Conclusion: VPQ提供了一个端到端、基于学习且理论上有保障的密钥生成框架，将量化与隐私放大统一到密钥速率目标的优化中，在泄露控制和有限块长性能上均优于现有方法。

Abstract: Secret key generation from correlated observations at Alice and Bob, in the presence of an eavesdropper Eve, underpins physical-layer security. Classical pipelines quantize by hand, amplify privacy afterwards, and optimize no objective tied to a key rate. We propose Variational Probabilistic Quantization (VPQ): neural encoders that map the correlated sources directly into a discrete key alphabet, trained by a variational adversarial objective whose entropy, mismatch, and leakage terms match the three terms of the one-way secret key rate. A linear code-offset secure sketch then reconciles the encoder outputs into an identical key without a separate privacy amplification step. We prove that the VPQ losses lower-bound the one-way secret key capacity of the induced source, and derive in closed form the optimal worst-case key rate over the source class of a given alphabet size and mismatch probability, attained by finite-field linear sketches. On Gaussian fading channels, VPQ leaks less to a correlated eavesdropper than one classical and two recent learning-based baselines, and Reed--Solomon reconciliation operates within the predicted finite-blocklength gap.

</details>


### [250] [A Joint Power-Privacy Control Framework for Decentralized Learning over Heterogeneous Wireless Multicasting Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03245)
*Amir Ziaeddini,Yauhen Yakimenka,Joerg Kliewer*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 提出一种在无线多播网络中融合功率控制与差分隐私的分布式学习框架，通过功率分配策略在模型系数与注入高斯噪声之间分配有限发射功率，以兼顾学习性能与隐私泄漏，并证明算法可实现O(logT)的累积遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式学习框架在无线网络场景下往往未同时考虑功率控制与隐私保护；无线信道具有非对称增益、节点最大发射功率异构等特点，需要在每个客户端功率受限的前提下，保持规定的每轮最大隐私泄漏水平并完成联合模型训练。

Method: 采用行随机邻接矩阵刻画非对称信道增益与异构最大发射功率；通过显式功率分配策略在模型系数与注入高斯噪声之间分配有限的发射功率，以此施加差分隐私，联合调控学习性能与隐私泄漏。

Result: 算法被证明达到O(logT)的累积遗憾界；在CIFAR-10上（IID与非IID场景、不同隐私水平、客户端数量及图拓扑）进行实验，在各类设置下表现出强性能，并优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 所提算法在真实无线通信约束下能同时保证隐私与学习性能，证明了功率分配式差分隐私机制的有效性与优越性。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a decentralized learning framework that incorporates both power control and privacy guarantees. Specifically, we enable a set of clients in a wireless multicast network to jointly train a common model while maintaining a prescribed per-iteration maximum privacy leakage level. The communication network is represented by a rowstochastic adjacency matrix, allowing us to capture asymmetric channel gains as well as heterogeneous maximum transmit power levels. Differential privacy is enforced through an explicit powersplitting strategy that allocates each node's limited maximum transmit power between model coefficients and injected Gaussian noise, thereby jointly controlling learning performance and privacy leakage. We further prove that the proposed algorithm achieves a cumulative regret bound of O(logT), whereTdenotes the time horizon. To evaluate the practical performance of our approach, we perform comprehensive experiments on the CIFAR-10 dataset under both IID and non-IID data distributions, considering different privacy levels, diverse numbers of clients, and various graph topologies. The results demonstrate strong performance across the considered settings and improved performance over existing methods, highlighting the effectiveness of the proposed algorithm under realistic wireless communication constraints.

</details>


### [251] [Iterative Semantic Decoding for Short Block Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03256)
*Jiafu Hao,Chentao Yue,Mingcheng Nie,Branka Vucetic,Yonghui Li*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 提出一种迭代增强的语义接收机，用于噪声无线信道上自然语言文本传输，将信道译码与语言模型结合，通过字符级交织和语义反馈机制在AWGN信道上获得约1.5 dB的BLER增益，并保持高BLEU/ROUGE得分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统短分组码在噪声信道上传输自然语言文本时缺乏语义信息辅助，错误较多、可靠性有限。作者希望利用语言模型与信道译码的迭代交互来增强接收性能，从而提高文本传输的可靠性与语义保真度。

Method: 发送端：将每个句子经字符级交织器置换，分割成多个段，并分别用短分组码独立编码。接收端：设计由信道译码器和语言模型组成的迭代译码器，二者之间插入去交织器，将每个段内的突发译码错误分散到整个句子中。每轮迭代中，语言模型对信道译码输出进行去噪，并将那些经验证与信道观测一致的去噪字符作为语义信息反馈给信道译码器，用于下一轮迭代。

Result: 在SNLI语料库和AWGN信道上的仿真结果表明，所提接收机相比传统短分组编码取得约1.5 dB的BLER增益；在SNR超过1.0 dB时，BLEU和ROUGE得分维持在99%以上。

Conclusion: 将语言模型与信道译码进行迭代融合、并利用经过验证的语义反馈，可以显著提升自然语言文本在噪声无线信道上的传输性能，在实现较大BLER增益的同时保持良好的语义保真度，为语义通信接收机设计提供了有效方案。

Abstract: This paper proposes an iteratively enhanced semantic receiver for natural-language text transmission over noisy wireless channels using multiple short block codes. At the transmitter, each sentence is permuted by a character-level interleaver, partitioned into segments, and independently encoded by short block codes. At the receiver, we develop an iterative decoder consisting of a channel decoder and a language model, where a de-interleaver between them disperses the burst decoding errors within each segment across the sentence. In each iteration, the language model denoises the channel decoding output, and the denoised characters verified to be consistent with the channel observations are fed back to the channel decoder as semantic information for the next iteration. Simulation results on the Stanford Natural Language Inference (SNLI) corpus over the additive white Gaussian noise (AWGN) channel show that the proposed receiver achieves approximately 1.5 dB block error rate (BLER) gain over conventional short-block coding, while maintaining BLEU and ROUGE scores above 99% at SNRs beyond 1.0 dB.

</details>


### [252] [Finite-SNR Closed Form for the Square Marčenko-Pastur MIMO Capacity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03356)
*Mohamed Akrout,Robert W. Heath*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文针对IID单用户MIMO信道在方形收发天线比（ratio=1）下渐进容量的解析缺口，提出了一种经典三角参数化方法。该参数化消除了Marchenko-Pastur谱在原点处的边奇异性，将移动的注水截断阈值替换为单一角度，使SNR和容量均成为该角度的显式函数；通过扫描角度而非在每个工作点求解标量约束即可描绘容量曲线，并顺带给出了低SNR行为的闭式结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对于IID单用户MIMO信道（发射端已知完整CSIT），渐进每接收天线容量是Marchenko-Pastur律上的注水积分。除收发天线比等于1（方形情况）外，所有比值下的闭式解均已知；方形情况因Marchenko-Pastur支撑到达原点而表现不同——任意有限SNR下注水截断阈值都严格位于支撑内部，导致水位只能通过数值方式获得，存在解析缺口。

Method: 采用Marchenko-Pastur平方律的经典三角参数化方法：利用三角恒等式参数化谱密度，从而消除支撑边界（原点）处的奇异性，并将移动的注水截断阈值转化为单一角度变量。由此SNR和容量都成为该角度的显式函数，容量曲线通过扫掠角度即可完整描绘，无需在每个SNR工作点求解隐式标量约束。

Result: 成功闭合了方形天线比下的容量解析缺口：容量曲线可通过扫掠单一角度直接绘制；同时该参数化给出了低SNR行为的闭式表达式，其中容量关于SNR的斜率等于极限谱的上边缘值。

Conclusion: 所提出的三角参数化不仅补全了方形天线比情形下MIMO容量闭式解的空白（此前水位仅能数值求解），而且提供了一种更优雅的容量曲线描述方式（角度扫描代替逐点标量求解），并在低SNR极限下给出简洁的闭式斜率，揭示容量与谱上边缘的直接联系。

Abstract: For the canonical independent and identically distributed (IID) single-user multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) channel with full channel state information at the transmitter (CSIT), the asymptotic capacity per receive antenna is a waterfilling integral over the Marčenko-Pastur law, whose shape depends only on the ratio of transmit to receive antennas. Closed forms are known for every ratio other than the square one, and there only above a finite signal-to-noise ratio (SNR). The square ratio behaves differently because the Marčenko-Pastur support reaches the origin. The waterfilling cutoff then stays strictly inside the support at every finite SNR, and the water level has been available only numerically. This letter closes that gap with a classical trigonometric parametrization of the square law, which removes the edge singularity and replaces the moving cutoff by a single angle. Both the SNR and the capacity become explicit functions of that angle, so the capacity curve is traced by sweeping the angle rather than by solving a scalar constraint at each operating point. The same parametrization delivers the low-SNR behavior in closed form, where the slope of capacity in SNR equals the upper edge of the limiting spectrum.

</details>


### [253] [TokenComSR: Task-Sensitivity-Guided Token Communication for Wireless Image Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03735)
*Ye Wang,Li Qiao,Zhen Gao,Hua Wang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 提出TokenComSR，一种面向资源受限无线图像传输的令牌通信框架，结合Swin Transformer令牌收发机、任务敏感功率分配（TSPA）与SNR条件令牌细化模块（TRM）及接收端超分辨率模块，在保真度和感知质量上均优于分离编码和JSCC-SR基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统分离编码在带宽受限衰落信道上存在悬崖效应；基于CNN的深度联合源信道编码（JSCC）虽能缓解该问题，但难以保留补丁级结构，从而无法实现自适应的逐令牌功率分配，也限制了令牌域内对超分辨率（SR）的补偿。

Method: 提出TokenComSR框架，包含任务敏感功率分配（TSPA）模块和SNR条件令牌细化模块（TRM）。TSPA将训练中估计的任务敏感度蒸馏为推理时的令牌功率权重；TRM估计SNR条件下的残差，以在解码前于令牌域纠正信道引入的失真。框架以Swin Transformer令牌收发机搭配接收端SR模块。

Result: 仿真结果验证了TSPA与TRM的有效性，在重建保真度和感知质量两方面均优于分离编码及JSCC-SR基线。

Conclusion: TokenComSR通过令牌域功率分配与SNR条件细化，有效缓解悬崖效应并保留补丁级结构，为资源受限无线边缘设备的图像传输提供了优于现有方案的联合源信道编码+超分辨率解决方案。

Abstract: For resource-constrained wireless edge devices over bandwidth-limited fading channels, wireless image transmission using traditional separate coding suffers from the cliff-effect collapse. Prevailing deep joint source-channel coding (JSCC) based on convolutional neural networks can mitigate this issue but usually fail to preserve patch-level structures, thereby preventing adaptive per-token power allocation and limiting token-domain compensation for super-resolution (SR). To address these challenges, we propose a token communication framework with SR (TokenComSR). Specifically, we conceive a task-sensitive power allocation (TSPA) module and a signal-to-noise ratio (SNR)-conditioned token refinement module (TRM). TSPA distills training estimates of task sensitivity into inference token power weights, while TRM estimates an SNR-conditioned residual to correct channel-induced distortion in the token domain before decoding. Building on TSPA and TRM, the proposed TokenComSR pairs a Swin Transformer-based token transceiver with a receiver-side SR module for resource-constrained wireless image transmission. Simulation results confirm the effectiveness of the proposed TSPA and TRM, demonstrating improvements over separate coding and JSCC-SR baselines in both reconstruction fidelity and perceptual quality.

</details>


### [254] [A Quantization Problem Posed by Adaptive Streaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03745)
*Yuriy A. Reznik*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文证明自适应比特率（ABR）流媒体中的编码阶梯（encoding ladder）选择问题本质上是一种特殊的标量量化问题：客户端逻辑将每个量化单元的修复值固定在单元左边缘，失真度量是单侧质量损失而非平方误差。作者推导出最优n档阶梯的质量间隙以Θ(1/n)衰减（而非教科书式的Θ(1/n²)），并给出极限常数C_w，以及码率放置（密度√(pQ')）、档位数n(ε)和经济最优阶梯规模n*等闭式设计规则，并推广到多分辨率、多编解码器、不同感知质量指标及二维自适应逻辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ABR流媒体是大多数互联网视频的传输技术，每个视频标题以多个码率（编码阶梯）编码，客户端选择网络带宽可支持的最高档位。此前编码阶梯的选择缺乏严格的理论基础，本文旨在为该问题建立量化理论框架，推导最优阶梯的结构与性能，并提供实用的阶梯设计规则。

Method: 作者将编码阶梯选择问题建模为带宽分布上的标量量化问题，但此量化具有特殊性：客户端逻辑将每个量化单元的重现值固定在单元左边缘（即只播放不高于带宽的码率），失真度量为单侧质量损失而非平方误差。基于该约束重塑经典量化理论，推导最优n档阶梯的质量间隙衰减律，并通过极值分析得到极限常数及最优码率密度（√(pQ')，取代Panter–Dite的p^{1/3}）。分析还扩展至多分辨率、多编解码器、不同质量度量及二维自适应逻辑。

Result: 最优n档阶梯的质量间隙G_n^*以Θ(1/n)衰减（而非经典量化的Θ(1/n²)）；当n很大时，n·G_n^*收敛于C_w=½(∫√(pQ')dB)²，且当阶梯码率服从密度√(pQ')时达到该极限（其中p为带宽密度，Q为内容质量-码率曲线）。由此得到闭式设计规则：分位数阶梯放置、达到容差ε所需档位数n(ε)≈C_w/ε，以及考虑运营成本κ和质量间隙定价λ时的经济最优阶梯规模n*=√(λC_w/κ)。

Conclusion: 编码阶梯设计可被严格表述为一种非经典标量量化问题，其单侧失真特性使最优间隙以Θ(1/n)而非Θ(1/n²)衰减。该理论给出了统一且简洁的闭式设计法则，并适用于不同分辨率、编解码器、质量指标和现代播放器二维自适应逻辑，为ABR阶梯的工程设计与经济权衡提供了理论基础。

Abstract: In adaptive bitrate (ABR) streaming, the delivery technology behind most Internet video, each title is encoded at several bitrates, forming an \emph{encoding ladder} of \emph{renditions}. Each client plays the highest rendition that its network bandwidth can sustain. We show that choosing the ladder is a problem of \emph{scalar quantization} of the bandwidth distribution. However, this quantization problem is of an unusual kind: the client's logic pins each quantization cell's reproduction value to the cell's \emph{left edge}, and the distortion measure is a one-sided quality loss rather than a squared error. The constraint reshapes the classical theory. The \emph{quality gap} of a ladder is the distance between its average delivered quality and the \emph{quality limit} that infinitely many renditions would attain. For optimal $n$-rendition ladders the gap $\mathcal{G}_n^*$ decays as $Θ(1/n)$, not the textbook $Θ(1/n^2)$. Moreover, with large $n$, $n\,\mathcal{G}_n^*\to C_w=\frac{1}{2}(\int\!\sqrt{p\,Q'}\,\,d B)^2$, with the limit attained when the ladder's rates follow the density $\sqrt{p\,Q'}$, which replaces Panter--Dite's $p^{1/3}$; here $p$ is the bandwidth density and $Q$ the quality--rate curve of the content. The law yields closed-form design rules: quantile rung placement, the rendition count $n(\varepsilon)\approx C_w/\varepsilon$ needed to reach tolerance $\varepsilon$, and the economic ladder size $n^*=\sqrt{λC_w/κ}$ when a rendition costs $κ$ to operate and the quality gap is priced at $λ$. The same analysis extends to the design of ladders employing different video resolutions, codecs, perceptual quality metrics, and the two-dimensional adaptation logic of modern web players.

</details>


### [255] [Hierarchical Beam Training and Codebook Design for Movable Antenna-Assisted Near-Field Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03776)
*Meihui Liu,Qian Zhang,Xuejun Cheng,Yuhui Jiao,Yunxiao Li,Ju Liu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 针对6G近场通信中波束训练开销过大的问题，本文提出一种基于可移动天线（MA）辅助近场系统的分层波束训练策略，并设计了相应的分层码本，该码本在角度-距离联合域上形成聚焦波束，在最大化目标区域波束增益的同时抑制向非目标区域的能量泄漏，仿真结果表明其相比传统固定位置天线系统和远场波束训练方法具有显著的性能优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6G通信向更高频段和更大阵列孔径演进，近场范围迅速扩展，使近场信道估计愈发重要且具有挑战性。波束训练虽是获取CSI的有效手段，但由于球面波传播特性，需要在角度和距离域进行联合搜索，导致波束训练开销难以承受。

Method: 利用可移动天线（MA）技术灵活重构天线位置，充分利用无线信道的空间变化以实现更精确的波束聚焦；在此基础上针对MA辅助近场系统设计了一种分层波束训练策略，并结合相应分层码本：该码本在角度-距离联合域上形成聚焦波束，在目标区域内最大化波束增益，同时抑制向非目标区域的能量泄漏。

Result: 仿真结果证实，该方法的波束增益和训练开销性能显著优于传统的固定位置天线（FPA）系统以及远场波束训练方法，实现了低训练开销与高波束增益的兼顾。

Conclusion: 研究表明，MA技术为近场高效波束训练设计提供了额外的灵活性，所提出的分层波束训练策略与分层码本能够有效解决近场联合角度-距离搜索带来的训练开销问题，是6G近场信道估计中一个有前景的解决方案。

Abstract: As sixth-generation (6G) communication systems evolve toward higher frequency bands and larger array apertures, the near-field range expands rapidly, making near-field channel estimation increasingly important and challenging. Beam training has been recognized as an effective approach for channel state information (CSI) acquisition. However, because of the propagation characteristics of spherical waves, beam training needs to perform a joint search in the angle and distance domains, which results in unaffordable beam training overhead. By flexibly reconfiguring antenna positions, movable antenna (MA) technology can fully exploit the spatial variations of wireless channels and achieve more accurate beam focusing, thereby providing additional flexibility for efficient beam training design. Therefore, based on MA-assisted near-field systems, we develop a hierarchical beam training strategy that combines reduced training overhead with high beam gain and design a corresponding hierarchical codebook. This codebook forms focused beams over the joint angle-distance domain, maximizing beam gain within the target region while suppressing energy leakage into non-target regions. Simulation results confirm substantial performance gains of the method over the conventional fixed-position antenna (FPA) system and the far-field beam training method.

</details>


### [256] [On the Impact of Site-Specific Training for a Real-World 5G NR System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04004)
*Reinhard Wiesmayr,Nuri Berke Baytekin,Chris Dick,Christoph Studer*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了无线接收机的站点专用微调（site-specific finetuning），在ETH Zurich的标准5G NR测试平台上，针对全可训练神经网络、模型驱动神经网络和基于模型的接收机三种架构进行微调评估，并基于间隔六个月以上的实测数据验证其有效性。结果显示微调能显著提升两类神经接收机、对模型接收机增益有限，且联合微调的单/双层接收机能媲美分别微调的性能，并在跨六个月的数据中保持有效。此外，结合迭代检测译码的站点专用LMMSE信道估计取得了最低的误码率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 站点专用训练可以在不增加计算复杂度的前提下提升无线接收机性能，但此前真实世界的研究仅局限于完全可训练的神经接收机和单层传输。本文旨在探究在更实际、更复杂的标准兼容5G NR双上行链路传输场景下，站点专用微调对不同接收机架构的适用性和收益，并验证其在长时间跨度数据上的稳定性。

Method: 作者采用ETH Zurich的标准兼容5G NR测试平台，收集包含双上行链路传输且相隔六个月以上的新测量数据，对三种接收机架构（完全可训练神经网络、模型驱动神经网络、基于模型）分别进行站点专用微调，并对比单、双层传输的联合微调与分别微调；同时考察了基于合成信道或站点实测数据估计协方差矩阵的站点专用LMMSE信道估计，并将其与迭代检测译码相结合。

Result: 实验表明：站点专用微调大幅提升完全可训练与模型驱动神经接收机的性能，而对可调参数较少的基于模型接收机仅带来微弱增益；联合微调的单神经接收机在单/双层传输上接近分别微调的接收机；微调效果在相隔六个月以上的测量数据上依然保持。站点专用信道估计结合迭代检测译码在数据集中取得了最低的误码率。

Conclusion: 站点专用微调是提升实际无线接收机性能的有效手段，尤其适用于参数化程度高的神经接收机架构，并具备跨时间的泛化能力；将站点特定信道统计信息纳入估计与迭代检测相结合可进一步逼近最优性能。作者公开了微调代码与数据集，以促进可复现研究。

Abstract: Site-specific training can improve wireless receiver performance without increasing computational complexity. However, real-world results have so far focused on fully trainable neural receivers and single-layer transmissions. We study site-specific finetuning of three receiver architectures: fully trainable neural, model-driven neural, and model-based. We train and evaluate these receivers using new measurements from a standard-compliant 5G NR testbed at ETH Zurich with dual-layer uplink transmission, including measurement campaigns conducted more than six months apart. Our results show that site-specific finetuning (i) substantially improves fully trainable and model-driven neural receivers, while resulting in only marginal gains for the less tunable model-based receiver; (ii) enables a single neural receiver jointly finetuned for single- and dual-layer transmission to closely match receivers finetuned separately for each configuration; and (iii) remains effective across measurement campaigns separated by more than six months. We also investigate site-specific linear minimum mean-square error channel estimation using covariance matrices estimated from either synthetic channels or site-specific measurements. When combined with iterative detection and decoding, site-specific channel estimation achieves the lowest error rate observed in our datasets. Our finetuning code and measurement datasets are publicly available at https://github.com/IIP-Group/site_specific_training

</details>


### [257] [Synchronization Strings over the Optimal Alphabet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04122)
*Huibo Xu,Shi Fu,Youming Qiao,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文彻底解决了同步字符串（synchronization strings）的最小常量字母表大小问题：证明了三元字母表即可实现任意长度的同步字符串（长度为任意 n 的 2001/2002-同步字符串），并通过计算机辅助构造将结果改进为对所有 epsilon > 226/227 成立。核心创新是一个"局部熵转移定理"：任何具有正条件最小熵的平方自由块局部信源都能支撑固定间隙的同步字符串；结合 Brinkhuis 族的逐次出现分支、删除球估计与不对称 Lovász 局部引理完成证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同步字符串为数据流附上确定性的位置标签，使其在插入与删除操作下仍然可靠。此前已知四个符号（在某固定 epsilon<1 下）足以，而两个符号无法支持任意长度的同步字符串，因此三个符号这一剩余情形成为悬而未决的关键问题。解决三元情形即可确定最小常量字母表大小的精确值，这是该领域的核心开放问题。

Method: 主要技术贡献是"局部熵转移定理"：每个具有正条件最小熵的平方自由块局部信源都支持固定间隙的同步字符串。具体实现路径为：利用 Brinkhuis 族中的逐次出现分支（occurrence-wise branching），使每次选择都保持三元且平方自由，同时保证任何长区间在暴露其外部所有选择后仍保留线性条件最小熵；再用删除球估计将该熵转化为关于高最长公共子序列的反集中性；最后通过非对称 Lovász 局部引理同时强制执行所有区间约束。该框架还自然导出指数多个合法词、Las Vegas 多项式时间构造、同步圆以及一类极值平方自由词内的同步。

Result: 证明了对任意长度都存在三元 2001/2002-同步字符串，从而确定三元是最小常量字母表大小；计算机辅助的 54-均匀族精细化进一步得到对所有 epsilon > 226/227 成立的三元 epsilon-同步字符串。作为副产品，还获得指数多个合法词、Las Vegas 多项式时间构造算法、同步圆，以及在极值平方自由词类内的同步能力。

Conclusion: 三元是同步字符串的最小常量字母表大小，精确回答了此前四符号可行、两符号不可行的剩余情形。所提出的局部熵转移框架不仅解决了三元情形的存在性问题，还提供了多项式时间构造、指数多个实例及同步圆等更广泛的结果，为该领域奠定了新的统一技术基础。

Abstract: Synchronization strings attach deterministic position labels to a stream that survive insertions and deletions. Haeupler and Shahrasbi introduced these objects, and subsequent work proved that four symbols suffice for some fixed parameter epsilon < 1, whereas two symbols cannot support arbitrarily long synchronization strings. We resolve the remaining ternary case: every length admits a ternary 2001/2002-synchronization string. Thus three is the exact minimum constant alphabet size. A computer-assisted refinement based on a larger 54-uniform family yields ternary epsilon-synchronization strings for every epsilon > 226/227.
  The previous four-symbol construction uses a ternary square-free backbone to exclude short repetitions and a fourth symbol to carry long-range synchronization marks. Our main technical contribution is a local-entropy transfer theorem: every square-free block-local source with positive conditional min-entropy supports synchronization strings with a fixed gap. We instantiate this theorem using occurrence-wise branching in a Brinkhuis family. Every outcome remains ternary and square-free, while every long interval retains linear conditional min-entropy after all choices outside it are exposed. A deletion-ball estimate converts this entropy into anti-concentration for high longest common subsequences, and an asymmetric Lovasz Local Lemma enforces all interval constraints simultaneously. The same framework also yields exponentially many valid words, a Las Vegas polynomial-time construction, synchronization circles, and synchronization within a class of extremal square-free words.

</details>


<div id='cs.CE'></div>

# cs.CE [[Back]](#toc)

### [258] [Markovian Shock-Source Tracing and Multidimensional Asset Roles in Exchange Rates, Gold Futures, and Bitcoin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03437)
*Seung Ho Choi,Seoin Jang,Hyunwoo Lee,Hayoung Choi*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本研究构建包含主要汇率、黄金期货和比特币的国际金融网络，利用VAR-GFEVD估计收益溢出，并将正成对净溢出结构映射为行随机马尔可夫核，从三个互补维度（直接溢出传输、平稳源追踪动态、多步上游连通性）刻画资产角色，发现黄金期货是主导直接净发射端与平稳源追踪节点，但并非病毒中心性最高者；一些净接收汇率节点具有较高的病毒中心性，比特币居于中间角色，说明净溢出无法单独推断三类连通性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的净发射端—净接收端二分法只能提供单一的总体角色刻画，难以揭示金融网络中资产的复杂多维角色，例如某资产虽为净接收端却可能条件性地广泛触达上游源节点。因此需要超越净溢出指标，引入直接溢出传输、平稳源追踪和多步上游连通性三个互补维度，以更准确地刻画资产在扩散过程中的角色。

Method: 使用VAR广义预测误差方差分解（VAR-GFEVD）估计收益溢出；将正成对净溢出结构映射为行随机马尔可夫核，其转移沿原始传输边的反方向追踪主导净溢出源；用平稳离开通量描述源追踪链的长期运动；并采用Fink等人的确定性概率传播算法计算病毒中心性（Viral Centrality），近似多步上游可达性。同时以美元指数作为辅助设定检验结果稳健性。

Result: 实证显示：黄金期货是主导的直接净发射端和领先的平稳源追踪节点，但并非病毒中心性最大者；多个被归类为直接净接收端的汇率节点具有较高的病毒中心性，表明其对上游源节点存在广泛的条件性访问；比特币居于中间角色；加入美元指数的辅助设定得到性质相似的角色分化。

Conclusion: 直接连通性、平稳源追踪与近似多步上游可达性无法仅由净溢出（NET spillover）推断得出，三个维度刻画了资产在网络中互补且各异的角色。所有扩散解释均为描述性的，且以估计网络、行归一化及重启闭合为条件。

Abstract: This study examines cross-asset connectedness in an international financial network of major exchange rates, gold futures, and Bitcoin. Moving beyond the conventional net transmitter--receiver classification, we characterize asset roles through three complementary dimensions: direct spillover transmission, stationary source-tracing dynamics, and multistep upstream connectivity. Return spillovers are estimated using VAR generalized forecast-error variance decomposition (VAR-GFEVD). The positive pairwise net-spillover structure is then mapped into a row-stochastic Markov kernel whose transitions trace dominant net-spillover sources in the reverse direction of the original transmission edges. A stationary departure flux describes long-run movement in this source-tracing chain, while Viral Centrality is evaluated by the deterministic probability-propagation algorithm of Fink et al. to approximate multistep upstream reach. Empirically, gold futures emerge as the dominant direct net transmitter and the leading stationary source-tracing node, but do not have the largest Viral Centrality. Several exchange-rate nodes classified as direct net receivers have relatively high Viral Centrality, indicating broad conditional access to upstream source nodes. Bitcoin occupies an intermediate role. An auxiliary specification including the U.S. Dollar Index yields qualitatively similar role differentiation. The results show that direct connectedness, stationary source tracing, and approximate multistep upstream reach cannot be inferred from NET spillovers alone. All diffusion interpretations are descriptive and conditional on the estimated network, row normalization, and restart closure.

</details>


### [259] [Calibration of neural viscoelastic models via full-field data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03645)
*Brain M. Riemer,Markus Kästner,Karl A. Kalina*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无监督学习框架，利用全场数据（仅需实验中可直接获取的全局反作用力和表面位移）来标定小应变粘弹性物理增强神经网络（PANN）。该网络嵌入广义标准材料理论，通过两个标量势函数保证热力学一致性，并利用基于不变量的表示保证材料对称性。在平面应力假设下，结合平衡间隙方法、拟牛顿优化器和自动微分构建约束优化问题；使用隐式时间积分和局部牛顿迭代求解非线性方程组；并采用伴随方法高效计算损失梯度。在含噪声的合成数据上，该方法在宽泛的变形速率和加载路径下均表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上标定粘弹性本构模型需要大量实验数据或难以测量的内部量；而基于神经网络的力学模型训练往往需要完整内场数据或对实验条件要求苛刻。本文旨在构建一个仅依赖实际实验中可直接测量的量（全局反作用力与表面位移）的无监督标定框架，同时保证模型满足热力学一致性和材料对称性，并降低训练的计算成本。

Method: 该方法基于广义标准材料理论，将PANN表示为自由能和双耗散势两个标量势函数，从而从构造上保证热力学一致性与材料对称性。在平面应力薄试样假设下，采用平衡间隙方法构建约束优化问题，结合拟牛顿优化器与自动微分求解。未知的离面应变由平面应力条件确定，内部变量演化通过隐式时间积分捕捉，并在每个积分点和时间步内用局部牛顿迭代求解非线性方程组。为降低训练成本，梯度采用后向伴随方法计算，而非对所有牛顿迭代步骤反向传播。

Result: 在含噪声位移和力的合成数据上验证了该框架，结果表明在广泛的变形速率范围和加载路径下，标定出的PANN模型与真实行为高度吻合，验证了方法的准确性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该无监督框架仅依靠实验中可直接测量的量即可可靠地标定热力学一致、材料对称的粘弹性PANN模型，且伴随方法显著降低了训练计算开销，为将物理增强神经网络用于实际实验数据标定提供了可行且高效的途径。

Abstract: We propose an unsupervised learning framework for calibrating a physics-augmented neural network (PANN) for small-strain viscoelasticity via full-field data. It only requires quantities that are directly accessible in real experiments for training, namely global reaction forces and surface displacements. The underlying PANN is embedded in the generalized standard materials theory, in which two scalar-valued potentials render the constitutive model thermodynamically consistent by construction, while invariant-based representations of the free energy and the dual dissipation potential additionally ensure material symmetry. Considering a thin specimen under the plane stress assumption, we formulate a constrained optimization problem based on the equilibrium gap method in combination with quasi-Newton optimizers and automatic differentiation. Thereby, the unknown out-of-plane strain follows from the plane stress condition and the evolution of the internal variables is captured by an implicit time integration scheme. The resulting system of nonlinear equations is solved via a local Newton iteration at quadrature point and time step. To drastically reduce the computational cost of training, the backward adjoint method is employed to compute the gradient of the target loss, instead of backpropagating through all Newton iteration steps. The proposed framework is demonstrated for synthetic data, including noisy displacements and forces, showing excellent agreement across a wide range of deformation rates and load paths.

</details>


### [260] [What Do CAE Simulation Agents Really Need Beyond a Generic Harness?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03718)
*Jiasheng Shi,Tianhan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文研究CAE仿真智能体在通用LLM智能体框架（多轮推理、工具使用、执行反馈）已具备的条件下，还需要哪些额外能力。结果表明：在信息访问和修复预算固定的前提下，单智能体通用框架即可匹敌甚至超越多智能体专用系统（FoamBench 96.4% vs 88.2%）；执行反馈修复带来最大增益（71.8%→96.4%），脚本化反思无额外收益，而唯一仍有帮助的输入是以求解器教程形式提供的领域知识（80.9%→96.4%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CAE仿真求解器（如OpenFOAM、FEniCS、COMSOL）的搭建高度依赖专家经验，LLM智能体有望将自然语言请求直接转化为可运行的仿真。已有CAE智能体引入了多智能体分解、领域检索和脚本化反思等专用机制，但这些机制是为较弱的基座模型设计的；现代通用智能体框架本身已提供多轮推理、工具使用和执行反馈。因此需要回答：在通用框架之上，CAE仿真智能体究竟还需要什么？

Method: 在信息访问和修复预算保持固定的条件下，将单智能体通用框架（利用现代harness自带的多轮推理、工具使用和执行反馈）与多智能体专用CAE系统进行对比，并在FoamBench基准上通过消融实验分别检验执行反馈修复、脚本化反思以及以求解器教程形式注入领域知识这三类因素的作用。

Result: 单智能体通用框架在FoamBench上达到96.4%的成功率，优于多智能体专用系统的88.2%。消融显示：执行反馈修复使成绩从71.8%（无修复轮次）提升到96.4%；脚本化反思没有额外贡献；而以求解器教程形式提供的领域知识是最大的单点增益来源，将成绩从80.9%提升到96.4%。

Conclusion: 现代通用智能体框架已经覆盖了此前CAE专用智能体靠多智能体分解和脚本化反思获得的收益；CAE仿真智能体的关键短板在于领域知识，为通用框架补充求解器教程形式的领域知识即可获得最大提升，无需额外设计复杂的专用机制。

Abstract: Computer-aided engineering (CAE) simulation is among the largest and most demanding areas of engineering, where setting up a solver such as OpenFOAM, FEniCS, or COMSOL takes real expertise.
